A cookie is a text file that is stored on your device. We use these text files for functionality such as to analyze our traffic or to personalize content.</p><p> </p><p>You can easily control how we use cookies on your device by adjusting the settings below, and you may also change those settings at any time by visiting our <a href="https://saabgroup.com/site-info/terms-of-use/" target="_blank">cookie policy page.</a></p><p> </p><p>Remember, if you <h3>Testing new 3D print technology develops opportunities</h3>
<div class="text-container">
<p>When a fighter gets damaged in battle, the pilot must typically get the jet back to base for repair. But a new project at Saab is examining how 3D printing can be used to repair battle damaged fighters in the field.</p>
</div>
<ul class="links-in-list">
<li>
<a class="external" href="/stories/2019-08/testing-new-technology-develops-opportunities/" title="Go to story">Go to story</a>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>

<h3>Saab and Centropy investigate new ways of cooling electronics</h3>
<div class="text-container">
<p>During spring, Saab has engaged in a collaboration with the startup Centropy in order to research new ways of cooling electronics. The first proof-of-concept is now being finalized and findings and lessons learned are now being implemented both in Saab and Centropy.</p>
</div>

<li>
<a class="external" href="/stories/2019-07/saab-and-centropy-investigate-new-ways-of-cooling-electronics/" title="Read more">Read more</a>

</a>

<h3>How Saab’s Sea Giraffe AMB was selected for six classes of U.S ships</h3>
<div class="text-container">
<p><span lang="EN-GB">With radars selected for six classes of U.S. ships, Saab's Sea Giraffe is truly the go-to naval radar for the U.S. Navy. Jay Abendroth, head of Saab's U.S. Sensor Division, gives his view on the key to Saab’s U.S. success.</span></p>

<h3>Flexible, precise and reliable</h3>
<div class="text-container">
<p>With a range of 150 km, the ability to hit targets across a wide area, and a precision so high it can hit the target within a radius of one meter. Saab and Boeing have together developed the Ground-Launched Small Diameter Bomb (GLSDB) – the rocket artillery munition designed to meet the evolving needs of the armed forces.</p>

<div class="news-list all-items hidden-desktop">
<ul data-template-articletype="47" data-template-blockid="19054" data-template-contentloader="news" data-template-listcount="4" data-template-liststart="3" data-template-pageid="17155" data-template-source="41109" data-template-type="list">
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/news-and-media/2019-10/saab-and-v-systems-launch-game-changing-enhanced-flight-vision-system-solution/" itemprop="url">
								Saab and Vū Systems Launch Game-changing Enhanced Flight Vision System Solution
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">
<span class="date" content="10/22/2019 9:00:00 AM" itemprop="datePublished">22 October 2019</span>
</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>Saab and Vū Systems are launching a Next Generation Enhanced Flight Vision System (EFVS) solution using new passive millimetre wave (PMMW) technology, enabling pilots to see and land in low-visibility conditions at all times.
			                </p></div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/news-and-media/2019-10/saabs-results-january-september-2019/" itemprop="url">
								Saab’s Results January-September 2019
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">
<span class="date" content="10/22/2019 7:30:00 AM" itemprop="datePublished">22 October 2019</span>
</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>Saab presents the results for January-September 2019.
			                </p></div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/news-and-media/2019-10/saab-announces-new-3d-global-terrain-database-solution-for-aeronautical-applications/" itemprop="url">
								Saab Announces new 3D Global Terrain Database Solution for Aeronautical Applications
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">
<span class="date" content="10/21/2019 11:00:00 PM" itemprop="datePublished">21 October 2019</span>
</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>Saab today announced a new aeronautical database solution that will provide the aircraft industry the most advanced 3D global terrain and elevation data commercially available.
			                </p></div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/news-and-media/2019-10/hakan-buskhe-resigns-from-the-saab-board-of-directors/" itemprop="url">
								Håkan Buskhe resigns from the Saab Board of Directors
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">
<span class="date" content="10/21/2019 1:00:00 PM" itemprop="datePublished">21 October 2019</span>
</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>Håkan Buskhe has today notified the Board of Directors that his assignment as member of the Board of Saab AB will end in conjunction with him resigning from his position as Saab President and CEO on October 22, 2019.
			                </p></div>
</div>
</li>

<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/stories/2019-10/awarded-solution-on-display-at-nbaa/" itemprop="url">
								Awarded solution on display at NBAA
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">

</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>Recently Saab was honored with the Airbus Innovation Award for the new Overheat detection system (OHDS) currently being developed for Airbus A350. Meet us and learn more at NBAA, Las Vegas, October 22-24.</p>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
<div class="content">
<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">
<a data-template-property="LinkURL" data-template-property-attribute="href" data-template-type="property" href="/stories/2019-10/a-different-definition-of-bird-hunting/" itemprop="url">
								A different definition of bird hunting
							</a>
</h3>
<span class="info">
<span class="date" content="10/8/2019 3:38:53 PM" itemprop="datePublished">08 October 2019</span>
</span>
<div class="description" data-template-property="Introduction" data-template-type="property" itemprop="description">
<p>The image of the first Brazilian Gripen E during its first flight has been seen by a worldwide audience. We spoke to the man behind the picture – and what flying photography is all about. Enter the world of our colleague Linus Svensson.</p>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="js-linkblock no-image is-linked" data-template-type="item" itemscope="" itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">

<h3 data-template-find="a" data-template-property="Heading" data-template-type="property" itemprop="name headline">

<p><span>On 26 October Saab received an order to provide and integrate the combat system for the Finnish Navy’s new Pohjanmaa-class corvettes within the Squadron 2020 programme. Here is an overview of what this means. </span></p>
</div>
</div>
</li>
